# Freewill & Eternal Security

**[00:00:00]** Hey, what's up everybody?

**[00:00:26]** Welcome back to the show. My name is Tudor Alexander and this is the Dance of Life podcast.

**[00:00:31]** Welcoming you to the fifth episode in this series. It's the Once Saved, Always Saved series.

**[00:00:37]** This week we're talking about eternal security and free will. So it's a pretty hot topic, I guess.

**[00:00:44]** We're going to be talking about all kinds of really interesting things with free will.

**[00:00:50]** You know, this is such a big topic and pretty controversial within Christianity

**[00:00:56]** on the other side you have the camp that talks about predestination, which we opened up last week.

**[00:01:23]** So this week I wanted to,

**[00:01:30]** and, you know, free will and all the stuff that I had for free will, but it's just too much content.

**[00:01:36]** So, you know, these things are on the longer end because ultimately there's just so much to talk about with these topics, right?

**[00:01:43]** And so free will is, it's a heavy topic, right? Are we free? What is the nature of our experience here?

**[00:01:56]** How do we reconcile the two?
